   1++6DB3              ; ============================================================================
   2++6DB3              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6DB3              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6DB3              ;
   5++6DB3              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6DB3              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6DB3              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6DB3              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6DB3              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6DB3              ; ============================================================================
  11++6DB3              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6DB3              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6DB3              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6DB3              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6DB3              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6DB3              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6DB3
  18++6DB3              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6DB3                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6DB3 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6DB6 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6DB9 B7                   or      a
  23++6DBA ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6DBC C2 FB 6D             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6DBF
  26++6DBF                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6DBF 21 F7 92             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6DC2 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6DC5 B7                   or      a
  30++6DC6 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6DC8 DA FB 6D             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6DCB CA FB 6D             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6DCE 22 15 6E             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6DD1 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6DD2 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6DD4 D2 FB 6D             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6DD7
  38++6DD7 21 F7 92             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6DDA 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6DDD B7                   or      a
  41++6DDE ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6DE0 DA E9 6D             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6DE3 CA E9 6D             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6DE6 C3 FB 6D             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6DE9              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6DE9                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6DE9 3A A4 76             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6DEC FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6DEE C2 FB 6D             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6DF1 3A A1 76             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6DF4 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6DF6 C2 FB 6D             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6DF9 B7                   or      a
  54++6DFA C9                   ret
  55++6DFB
  56++6DFB              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6DFB 37                   scf
  58++6DFC C9                   ret
  59++6DFD
  60++6DFD              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6DFD              p127_layout_report:
  62++6DFD 21 17 6E             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6E00 CD F1 62             call    p127_puts
  64++6E03 3A 16 6E             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)
  65++6E06 CD 0D 63             call    p127_put_hex8
  66++6E09 3A 15 6E             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size)
  67++6E0C CD 0D 63             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6E0F 21 33 6E             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6E12 C3 F1 62             jp      p127_puts
  70++6E15
  71++6E15              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6E15 00 00                dw      0
  73++6E17              p127_msg_kernel_size:
  74++6E17 5B 50 31 32          db      "[P127] KERNEL IMAGE SIZE=0x",0
  74++6E1B 37 5D 20 4B
  74++6E1F 45 52 4E 45
  74++6E23 4C 20 49 4D
  74++6E27 41 47 45 20
  74++6E2B 53 49 5A 45
  74++6E2F 3D 30 78 00
  75++6E33              p127_msg_kernel_size_tail:

 440+ 6E4F                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6E4F              ; ============================================================================
   2++6E4F              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6E4F              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6E4F              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6E4F              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6E4F              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6E4F              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6E4F              ; ============================================================================
   9++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6E4F
  13++6E4F              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6E4F              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6E4F              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6E4F              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6E4F              p127_resident_start:
  18++6E4F
  19++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6E4F              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6E4F
  26++6E4F              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6E4F              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6E4F              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6E4F              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6E4F              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6E4F 32 D9 6E             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6E52 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6E53 32 DA 6E             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6E56 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6E57 32 DB 6E             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6E5A 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6E5B 32 DC 6E             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6E5E
  39++6E5E 3A D9 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6E61 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6E63 C2 8D 6E             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6E66 3A DA 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6E69 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6E6B C2 8D 6E             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6E6E 3A DB 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6E71 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6E73 C2 8D 6E             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6E76 3A DC 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6E79 E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6E7B CA 8D 6E             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6E7E                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6E7E 3A D9 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6E81 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6E83 CA 8D 6E             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6E86 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6E88 CA 8D 6E             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6E8B B7                   or      a
  58++6E8C C9                   ret
  59++6E8D
  60++6E8D              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6E8D 37                   scf
  62++6E8E C9                   ret
  63++6E8F
  64++6E8F              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6E8F              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6E8F              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6E8F 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6E91 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6E93 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6E95 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6E97 CD 4F 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6E9A DA D7 6E             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6E9D
  74++6E9D 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6E9F 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6EA1 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6EA3 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6EA5 CD 4F 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6EA8 D2 D7 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6EAB
  81++6EAB 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6EAD 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6EAF 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6EB1 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6EB3 CD 4F 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6EB6 D2 D7 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6EB9
  88++6EB9 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6EBB 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6EBD 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6EBF 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6EC1 CD 4F 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6EC4 D2 D7 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6EC7
  95++6EC7 3E 03                ld      a,3
  96++6EC9 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++6ECB 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++6ECD 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6ECF CD 4F 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++6ED2 D2 D7 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++6ED5 B7                   or      a
 102++6ED6 C9                   ret

 105++6ED7 37                   scf
 106++6ED8 C9                   ret

 109++6ED9 00                   db      0

 349++7080              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++7080              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++7080              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++7080              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++7080              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++7080              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++7080              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.

 383++70B1

 385++70B1                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.

   1++70CF              ; ============================================================================
   2++70CF              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++70CF              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++70CF              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++70CF              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++70CF              ; ============================================================================
   7++70CF              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++70CF              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++70CF              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++70CF              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++70CF              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++70CF              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++70CF
  14++70CF              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++70CF              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++70CF              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++70CF              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++70CF              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++70CF              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++70CF              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++70CF              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++70CF              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++70CF              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++70CF              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++70CF
  26++70CF              p127_irq_init:
  27++70CF F3                   di
  28++70D0 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++70D2 32 B1 73             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++70D5 AF                   xor     a
  31++70D6 32 B2 73             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++70D9 32 B3 73             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a

  34++70DF B7                   or      a
  35++70E0 C9                   ret
  36++70E1

  38++70E1              p127_irq_validate:

  40++70E4 FE 01                cp      P127_IRQ_MASKED

  48++70F9 B7                   or      a
  49++70FA C9                   ret

   1++73B5              ; ============================================================================
   2++73B5              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++73B5              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++73B5              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++73B5              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++73B5              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++73B5              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++73B5              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++73B5              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++73B5              ; ============================================================================
  11++73B5              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++73B5              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++73B5              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  14++73B5              P127_MEM_PAGE_COUNT    EQU 64
  15++73B5              P127_MEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  16++73B5              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0
  17++73B5              P127_MEM_RESERVED      EQU 1
  18++73B5              P127_MEM_ALLOCATED     EQU 2

   2++771F              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++771F              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++771F              ; ABI:
   5++771F              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++771F              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++771F              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++771F              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++771F              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++771F              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.

 444+ 7839                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_arena.asm"
# file opened: p127_arena.asm
   1++7839              ; ============================================================================
   2++7839              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++7839              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7839              ;
   5++7839              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++7839              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++7839              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++7839              ;
   9++7839              ; API:
  10++7839              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++7839              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++7839              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++7839              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++7839              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++7839              ;
  16++7839              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++7839              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++7839              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++7839              ; ============================================================================
  20++7839              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++7839              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++7839              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++7839              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++7839              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++7839
  26++7839              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++7839              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++7839              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++7839              p127_arena_init:
  30++7839 21 DE 76             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++783C 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++783E 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  33++7840              .p127_arena_init_scan:
  34++7840 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  35++7841 B7                   or      a
  36++7842 C2 4C 78             jp      nz,.p127_arena_init_guard_found
  37++7845 2B                   dec     hl
  38++7846 0D                   dec     c
  39++7847 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++7849 C3 FF 78             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++784C              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:
  42++784C 0C                   inc     c
  43++784D 79                   ld      a,c
  44++784E FE 40                cp      P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  45++7850 D2 FF 78             jp      nc,p127_arena_reject
  46++7853 32 90 79             ld      (p127_arena_low_page),a
  47++7856 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  48++7858 91                   sub     c
  49++7859 CA FF 78             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  50++785C 32 91 79             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a
  51++785F 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  52++7861 32 92 79             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++7864 B7                   or      a
  54++7865 C9                   ret
  55++7866
  56++7866              ; IN B=non-zero page count. OUT A=first page of the new block.
  57++7866              ; Validate the complete block before changing any page state.
  58++7866              p127_arena_alloc:
  59++7866 78                   ld      a,b
  60++7867 B7                   or      a
  61++7868 CA FF 78             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  62++786B 3A 92 79             ld      a,(p127_arena_next_page)
  63++786E 90                   sub     b
  64++786F DA FF 78             jp      c,p127_arena_reject
  65++7872 32 93 79             ld      (p127_arena_first_page),a
  66++7875 4F                   ld      c,a
  67++7876 3A 90 79             ld      a,(p127_arena_low_page)
  68++7879 5F                   ld      e,a
  69++787A 79                   ld      a,c
  70++787B 93                   sub     e
  71++787C DA FF 78             jp      c,p127_arena_reject
  72++787F 48                   ld      c,b
  73++7880 3A 93 79             ld      a,(p127_arena_first_page)
  74++7883 5F                   ld      e,a
  75++7884 16 00                ld      d,0
  76++7886 21 9F 76             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
  77++7889 19                   add     hl,de
  78++788A              .p127_arena_alloc_check:

   1++7997              ; ============================================================================
   2++7997              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++7997              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7997              ;
   5++7997              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++7997              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++7997              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++7997              ;
   9++7997              ; API:
  10++7997              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++7997              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++7997              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++7997              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++7997              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++7997              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++7997              ; ============================================================================
  17++7997              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++7997              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++7997              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++7997              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++7997              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++7997              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++7997

   1++7E18              ; ============================================================================
   2++7E18              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7E18              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7E18              ;
   5++7E18              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7E18              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7E18              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7E18              ;
   9++7E18              ; API inputs:
  10++7E18              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7E18              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7E18              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7E18              ; Outputs:
  14++7E18              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7E18              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7E18              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7E18              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7E18              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7E18              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7E18              ; ============================================================================
  21++7E18              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7E18              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7E18              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7E18              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7E18              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7E18

 177++84C6              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++84C6              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++84C6              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++84C6              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++84C6              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++84C6              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++84C6              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++84C6              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++84C6              p127_map_handoff_prepare:
 186++84C6                      ; Keep the target in E, invalidate any older staged request, then
 187++84C6                      ; validate the page/window and index the four-byte mask with DE=0:E.
 188++84C6 5F                   ld      e,a
 189++84C7 AF                   xor     a
 190++84C8 32 87 85             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 191++84CB 78                   ld      a,b
 192++84CC FE 40                cp      64
 193++84CE D2 8A 85             jp      nc,p127_map_handoff_reject
 194++84D1 7B                   ld      a,e
 195++84D2 FE 04                cp      P127_MAP_WINDOW_COUNT
 196++84D4 D2 8A 85             jp      nc,p127_map_handoff_reject
 197++84D7 16 00                ld      d,0
 198++84D9 21 83 85             ld      hl,p127_map_window_mask
 199++84DC 19                   add     hl,de
 200++84DD 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 201++84DE A1                   and     c
 202++84DF C2 8A 85             jp      nz,p127_map_handoff_reject
 203++84E2 7B                   ld      a,e
 204++84E3 32 88 85             ld      (p127_map_pending_window),a
 205++84E6 78                   ld      a,b
 206++84E7 32 89 85             ld      (p127_map_pending_page),a
 207++84EA 3E 01                ld      a,1
 208++84EC 32 87 85             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 209++84EF B7                   or      a
 210++84F0 C9                   ret
 211++84F1
 212++84F1              ; Cancel a staged request. No hardware state is modified.
 213++84F1              p127_map_handoff_abort:
 214++84F1 AF                   xor     a
 215++84F2 32 87 85             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 216++84F5 C9                   ret

   1++86BC              ; ============================================================================
   2++86BC              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++86BC              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++86BC              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++86BC              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++86BC              ; ============================================================================
   7++86BC              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++86BC              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++86BC              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++86BC
  11++86BC              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++86BC              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++86BC              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++86BC              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++86BC              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++86BC              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++86BC              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++86BC
  19++86BC              ; IN: none.
  20++86BC              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++86BC              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++86BC              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++86BC              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++86BC              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++86BC              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++86BC              p127_handoff_stack_probe:
  27++86BC F3                   di
  28++86BD F5                   push    af
  29++86BE C5                   push    bc

  37++86C9 21 00 00             ld      hl,0

  48++86DD 32 FF B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI),a

  51++86E0 31 80 B8             ld      sp,P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP

  53++86E6 E5                   push    hl
  54++86E7 11 68 24             ld      de,02468h
  55++86EA D5                   push    de
  56++86EB CD 3C 87             call    p127_handoff_stack_inner
  57++86EE DA 1C 87             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  58++86F1 D1                   pop     de

  60++86F5 B7                   or      a

  63++86FB E1                   pop     hl
  64++86FC 11 57 13             ld      de,01357h
  65++86FF B7                   or      a
  66++8700 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  67++8702 C2 1C 87             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  68++8705

  70++8708 FE A5                cp      0A5h
  71++870A C2 1C 87             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail

   2++8757              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader

# file opened: p127_speaker.asm
   1++8CA4              ; ============================================================================
   2++8CA4              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8CA4              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8CA4              ;
   5++8CA4              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8CA4              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8CA4              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8CA4              ;
   9++8CA4              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8CA4              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8CA4              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8CA4              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8CA4              ;
  14++8CA4              ; API:
  15++8CA4              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8CA4              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8CA4              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8CA4              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8CA4              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8CA4              ;
  21++8CA4              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8CA4              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8CA4              ; is assumed.
  24++8CA4              ; ============================================================================
  25++8CA4              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8CA4              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8CA4              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8CA4
  29++8CA4              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8CA4              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8CA4              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8CA4
  33++8CA4              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8CA4 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8CA6 32 02 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++8CA9 C9                   ret
  37++8CAA
  38++8CAA              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8CAA              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8CAA              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8CAA              p127_speaker_set:
  42++8CAA C5                   push    bc
  43++8CAB 47                   ld      b,a
  44++8CAC 3A 02 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++8CAF E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++8CB1 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++8CB2 78                   ld      a,b
  48++8CB3 E6 01                and     1
  49++8CB5 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++8CB7 79                   ld      a,c
  51++8CB8 F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++8CBA 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++8CBC              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++8CBC 79                   ld      a,c
  55++8CBD              p127_speaker_write:
  56++8CBD 32 02 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++8CC0 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++8CC2 C1                   pop     bc
  59++8CC3 C9                   ret
  60++8CC4
  61++8CC4              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8CC4              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++8CC4 3A 02 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++8CC7 EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++8CC9 32 02 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++8CCC D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8CCE C9                   ret
  68++8CCF
  69++8CCF              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++8CCF              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++8CCF 3A 02 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++8CD2 E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++8CD4 32 02 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++8CD7 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8CD9 C9                   ret
  76++8CDA
  77++8CDA              p127_speaker_get:
  78++8CDA 3A 02 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++8CDD C9                   ret
  80++8CDE
  81++8CDE
  82++8CDE              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++8CDE              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8CDE              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++8CDE              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++8CDE              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++8CDE F5                   push    af
  88++8CDF C5                   push    bc
  89++8CE0 D5                   push    de
  90++8CE1 E5                   push    hl
  91++8CE2 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++8CE4 CD A4 8C             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++8CE7 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++8CEA              p127_speaker_test_loop:
  95++8CEA CD C4 8C             call    p127_speaker_toggle
  96++8CED 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
  97++8CEF              p127_speaker_test_delay1:
  98++8CEF 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay1
  99++8CF1 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
 100++8CF3              p127_speaker_test_delay2:
 101++8CF3 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay2
 102++8CF5 1B                   dec     de
 103++8CF6 7A                   ld      a,d
 104++8CF7 B3                   or      e
 105++8CF8 20 F0                jr      nz,p127_speaker_test_loop
 106++8CFA CD CF 8C             call    p127_speaker_stop
 107++8CFD E1                   pop     hl
 108++8CFE D1                   pop     de
 109++8CFF C1                   pop     bc
 110++8D00 F1                   pop     af
 111++8D01 C9                   ret
 112++8D02
 113++8D02 00           p127_speaker_shadow: db 0
 114++8D03
# file closed: p127_speaker.asm

   2++8FD9              ; Project127 FAT16 root-directory reader

  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret
 128+ A081
 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:
 134+ A081
 135+ A081 F5                   push    af
 136+ A082 C5                   push    bc
 137+ A083 01 98 A0             ld      bc,p126_atm_restore_dosen_done
 138+ A086 C5                   push    bc
 139+ A087 01 53 2A             ld      bc,#2A53
 140+ A08A C5                   push    bc
 141+ A08B 01 77 01             ld      bc,#0177
 142+ A08E 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 143+ A090 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 144+ A093 3E C6                ld      a,#C6
 145+ A095 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
 146+ A098              p126_atm_restore_dosen_done:
 147+ A098 C1                   pop     bc
 148+ A099 F1                   pop     af
 149+ A09A C9                   ret
 150+ A09B
 151+ A09B
 152+ A09B              p127_ui_init:
 153+ A09B CD 11 A0             call p126_atm_video_init

 155+ A0A1 CD B8 A0             call BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
 156+ A0A4 CD 3E A1             call BIOS_DebugConsole_DrawInterface
 157+ A0A7 C9                   ret

 203+ A0FE ED B0                ldir

 207+ A108 01 FF 02             ld      bc, #02FF
 208+ A10B ED B0                ldir

 257+ A160 C1                   pop     bc
 258+ A161 F1                   pop     af
 259+ A162 C9                   ret

 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 262+ A163              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.
 263+ A163              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt:

 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc
 275+ A170 F1                   pop     af
 276+ A171 F5                   push    af
 277+ A172 C5                   push    bc
 278+ A173 D5                   push    de
 279+ A174 E5                   push    hl
 280+ A175 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 281+ A178 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 282+ A17B 79                   ld      a, c
 283+ A17C 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 286+ A182 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 292+ A18E 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 295+ A195 5F                   ld      e, a
 296+ A196 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 297+ A199 F5                   push    af
 298+ A19A 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 299+ A19D ED 79                out     (c),a
 300+ A19F F1                   pop     af
 301+ A1A0 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 302+ A1A3 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 303+ A1A6 3C                   inc     a
 304+ A1A7 FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 305+ A1A9 DA BA A1             jp      c, .p126_print_at_col_ok
 306+ A1AC AF                   xor     a
 307+ A1AD 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 308+ A1B0 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 309+ A1B3 3C                   inc     a
 310+ A1B4 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 311+ A1B7 C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 312+ A1BA              .p126_print_at_col_ok:

 314+ A1BD C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 315+ A1C0              .p126_print_at_done:

 317+ A1C3 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT

 320+ A1CA 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 321+ A1CC ED 79                out     (c),a
 322+ A1CE E1                   pop     hl
 323+ A1CF D1                   pop     de
 324+ A1D0 C1                   pop     bc
 325+ A1D1 F1                   pop     af

 329+ A1D4 D1                   pop     de

 332+ A1D7 C9                   ret

 334+ A1D8              ; IN: A=character. CR/LF/BS are handled; printable ASCII is rendered.

 356+ A1FF FE 80                cp      128
 357+ A201 DA 06 A2             jp      c, .p126_console_char_ok
 358+ A204 3E 3F                ld      a, '?'
 359+ A206              .p126_console_char_ok:

 361+ A209 3A 04 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_row)

   1+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A40C              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A40C              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A40C              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A40C              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A40C              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A40C
  15+ A40C              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A40C              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A40C              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A40C              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A40C              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A40C              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A40C              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A40C 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A40E CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace

 292+ A673
 293+ A673                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A673                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.
 295+ A673 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 296+ A676 5F                   ld      e,a
 297+ A677 16 00                ld      d,0
 298+ A679 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 299+ A67C 19                   add     hl,de
 300+ A67D              p127_physmem_full_verify_loop:
 301+ A67D 2B                   dec     hl
 302+ A67E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 303+ A67F 32 89 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_current_page),a
 304+ A682 3E 56                ld      a,'V'
 305+ A684 CD C0 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_page
 306+ A687 3A 89 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 307+ A68A CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 308+ A68D DA CE A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 309+ A690 3A 89 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 310+ A693 EE 5A                xor     05Ah
 311+ A695 57                   ld      d,a
 312+ A696 E5                   push    hl
 313+ A697 CD 66 AB             call    p127_physmem_verify_pattern
 314+ A69A E1                   pop     hl
 315+ A69B DA D8 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_data
 316+ A69E 3A 8A AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 317+ A6A1                      ; Reinitialize the reverse counter on first entry.
 318+ A6A1 B7                   or      a
 319+ A6A2 20 03                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready
 320+ A6A4 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 321+ A6A7              p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready:
 322+ A6A7 3D                   dec     a
 323+ A6A8 32 8A AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 324+ A6AB 20 D0                jr      nz,p127_physmem_full_verify_loop
 325+ A6AD
 326+ A6AD 3E 56                ld      a,'V'
 327+ A6AF CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
 328+ A6B2
 329+ A6B2                      ; Verify the allocator still marks every listed page ALLOCATED.
 330+ A6B2 CD 01 A9             call    p127_physmem_check_allocated

 345+ A6CE              p127_physmem_fail_map:

 368+ A6F1              p127_physmem_cleanup:
 369+ A6F1 3E 43                ld      a,'C'
 370+ A6F3 CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
 371+ A6F6                      ; Always return W3 to P0 before releasing UI ownership.
 372+ A6F6 3A 81 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_mapped)

 382+ A709 3A 86 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_saved_p0)

 384+ A70F
 385+ A70F              p127_physmem_cleanup_release:

 396+ A724 DA B9 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 397+ A727
 398+ A727              p127_physmem_cleanup_free:
 399+ A727 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 400+ A72A 32 8A AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 401+ A72D 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 402+ A730 5F                   ld      e,a
 403+ A731 16 00                ld      d,0

 407+ A737 3A 8A AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 408+ A73A B7                   or      a

 410+ A73D 2B                   dec     hl

 418+ A74B 32 8A AB             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 419+ A74E 18 E7                jr      p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop
 420+ A750              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A750                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.
 422+ A750 3A 7D AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 423+ A753 B7                   or      a
 424+ A754 20 21                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return
 425+ A756 CD 3F 74             call    p127_mem_alloc

 433+ A768 DA B9 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 434+ A76B CD C0 A8             call    p127_physmem_check_reserved
 435+ A76E DA B9 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 436+ A771 CD 20 A9             call    p127_physmem_check_free_state
 437+ A774 DA B9 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 438+ A777              p127_physmem_cleanup_return:
 439+ A777 3A 7D AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 440+ A77A B7                   or      a
 441+ A77B 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A77D                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A77D                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A77D                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.
 445+ A77D CD 84 A7             call    p127_physmem_replay_page_log
 446+ A780 B7                   or      a
 447+ A781 C9                   ret

 450+ A783 C9                   ret

 452+ A784              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A784              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A784              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A784                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A784                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A784                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A784                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A784 CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init

 461+ A787                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A787                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.

 575+ A8C0
 576+ A8C0              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8C0              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ A8C0              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ A8C0 21 9F 76             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ A8C3 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ A8C5 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ A8C7              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ A8C7 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ A8C8 FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A8CA 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8CC FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8CE 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8D0 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8D2 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8D4 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8D6 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8D8 FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8DA 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8DC FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8DE 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8E0 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8E2 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8E4 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8E6 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8E8 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip

 603+ A8EB E5                   push    hl

 610+ A8F6 E1                   pop     hl
 611+ A8F7 C1                   pop     bc
 612+ A8F8 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ A8FA              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:

 615+ A8FB 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ A8FD B7                   or      a
 617+ A8FE C9                   ret
 618+ A8FF              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ A8FF 37                   scf
 620+ A900 C9                   ret
 621+ A901
 622+ A901              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ A901              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ A901 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ A904 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ A905 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ A908              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ A908 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ A909 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ A90A 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ A90C C5                   push    bc
 632+ A90D E5                   push    hl
 633+ A90E 21 9F 76             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ A911 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ A912 7E                   ld      a,(hl)

 637+ A915 E1                   pop     hl

 640+ A919 23                   inc     hl
 641+ A91A 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ A91C B7                   or      a
 643+ A91D C9                   ret
 644+ A91E              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ A91E 37                   scf
 646+ A91F C9                   ret
 647+ A920
 648+ A920              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ A920              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ A920              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ A920 21 9F 76             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ A923 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ A925 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ A927              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ A927 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ A928 FE 00                cp      0
 657+ A92A 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ A92C FE 01                cp      1
 659+ A92E 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ A930 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ A932 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ A934 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ A936 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ A938 FE 04                cp      4
 665+ A93A 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ A93C FE 05                cp      5
 667+ A93E 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ A940 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ A942 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved

 671+ A945 B7                   or      a
 672+ A946 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ A948 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ A94A              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ A94A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ A94B FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ A94D 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ A94F              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ A94F 23                   inc     hl
 680+ A950 0C                   inc     c
 681+ A951 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop

 683+ A954 C9                   ret
 684+ A955              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ A955 37                   scf
 686+ A956 C9                   ret
 687+ A957
 688+ A957              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A957              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A957
 691+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A957              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A957              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A957              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A957              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A957              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A957              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A957              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A957              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A957              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A957              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A957 C3 3F 74             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A95A
 705+ A95A              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A95A 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A95D 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A960 B7                   or      a
 709+ A961 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A964 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A967 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A968 3A 93 85             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A96B B8                   cp      b
 714+ A96C CA 14 AA             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A96F 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A972 C3 55 74             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A975
 718+ A975              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A975              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A975 F3                   di
 721+ A976 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A979 B7                   or      a
 723+ A97A C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A97D 3A 93 85             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A980 B7                   or      a
 726+ A981 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A984 CD 0D 6F             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A987 D8                   ret     c
 729+ A988 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A98A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A98D CD 2C 6F             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A990 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A992 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A994 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A997 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A99A 32 B6 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A99D 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A99F 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A9A2 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A9A5 B7                   or      a
 741+ A9A6 C9                   ret
 742+ A9A7              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A9A7 CD 54 6F             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A9AA AF                   xor     a
 745+ A9AB 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A9AE 37                   scf
 747+ A9AF C9                   ret
 748+ A9B0
 749+ A9B0              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A9B0              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A9B0 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A9B3 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A9B6 FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A9B8 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9BB 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9BE FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9C0 D2 14 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9C3 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9C4 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9C6 21 9F 76             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9C9 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9CA 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9CB FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9CD C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9D0 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9D3 C3 40 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9D6
 768+ A9D6              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9D6              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9D6              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9D6 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9D9 FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9DB C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9DE AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9DF CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9E2 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9E3 3A B5 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 779+ A9E7 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9E9 3A B6 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9EC 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9EF              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9EF 3A B4 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9F2 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9F3 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9F5 CD 40 6F             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9F8 D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9F9 AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9FA 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9FD              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9FD 3A B3 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ AA00 B7                   or      a
 793+ AA01 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ AA03 CD 54 6F             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ AA06 D8                   ret     c
 796+ AA07 AF                   xor     a
 797+ AA08 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ AA0B              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ AA0B AF                   xor     a
 800+ AA0C 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ AA0F 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ AA12 B7                   or      a
 803+ AA13 C9                   ret
 804+ AA14
 805+ AA14              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ AA14 37                   scf
 807+ AA15 C9                   ret
 808+ AA16
 809+ AA16              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ AA16              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ AA16              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ AA16 AF                   xor     a
 813+ AA17 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA1A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA1D 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA20 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA23 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA25 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA28 D2 B0 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA2B CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA2E DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA31 32 B8 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA34 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA37 32 B9 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA3A CD 75 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA3D DA AA AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA40                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA40 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA42 CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA45 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA48 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA4B CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA4E DA A7 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA51                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA51 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA54 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA57 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA5A 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA5C 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA5F CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA62 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA65 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA68 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA69 3A B9 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA6C B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA6D C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA70 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA73 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA76 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA79                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA79 CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA7C DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA7F 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA80 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA83 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA84 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA87 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA88 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA8B DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA8E 3A 7A 70             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA91 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA93 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA96 3A 7B 70             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA99 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA9B C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA9E 3A 93 85             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AAA1 B7                   or      a
 868+ AAA2 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AAA5 B7                   or      a
 870+ AAA6 C9                   ret
 871+ AAA7
 872+ AAA7              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AAA7 CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AAAA              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AAAA 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AAAD CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AAB0              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AAB0 37                   scf
 879+ AAB1 C9                   ret
 880+ AAB2
 881+ AAB2 00           p127_physmem_api_active: db 0
 882+ AAB3 00           p127_physmem_api_quiesced: db 0
 883+ AAB4 00           p127_physmem_api_acquired: db 0
 884+ AAB5 00           p127_physmem_api_saved_valid: db 0
 885+ AAB6 00           p127_physmem_api_saved_p0: db 0

 888+ AAB9 00           p127_physmem_api_test_p0: db 0

 890+ AABA              p127_physmem_trace:
 891+ AABA 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 892+ AABD ED 79                out     (c),a
 893+ AABF C9                   ret
 894+ AAC0
 895+ AAC0              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AAC0              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AAC0              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AAC0              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AAC0 F5                   push    af
 900+ AAC1 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AAC2 D5                   push    de
 902+ AAC3 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AAC4 FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AAC6 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AAC8 21 12 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AACB 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ AACD              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ AACD 21 20 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ AAD0              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ AAD0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ AAD1 B7                   or      a
 912+ AAD2 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
 913+ AAD4 23                   inc     hl
 914+ AAD5 E5                   push    hl
 915+ AAD6 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 916+ AAD9 E1                   pop     hl
 917+ AADA 18 F4                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 918+ AADC              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:
 919+ AADC 3A 89 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 920+ AADF CD F1 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8
 921+ AAE2 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 922+ AAE4 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 923+ AAE7 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 924+ AAE9 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 925+ AAEC E1                   pop     hl
 926+ AAED D1                   pop     de
 927+ AAEE C1                   pop     bc
 928+ AAEF F1                   pop     af
 929+ AAF0 C9                   ret
 930+ AAF1
 931+ AAF1              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ AAF1 F5                   push    af
 933+ AAF2 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ AAF4 0F                   rrca
 935+ AAF5 0F                   rrca
 936+ AAF6 0F                   rrca
 937+ AAF7 0F                   rrca
 938+ AAF8 CD 01 AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 939+ AAFB F1                   pop     af
 940+ AAFC E6 0F                and     00Fh
 941+ AAFE C3 01 AB             jp      p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 942+ AB01
 943+ AB01              p127_physmem_trace_nibble:
 944+ AB01 5F                   ld      e,a
 945+ AB02 16 00                ld      d,0
 946+ AB04 21 30 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_hex_table
 947+ AB07 19                   add     hl,de
 948+ AB08 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 949+ AB09 C3 0C AB             jp      p127_physmem_trace_char
 950+ AB0C
 951+ AB0C              p127_physmem_trace_char:
 952+ AB0C 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 953+ AB0F ED 79                out     (c),a
 954+ AB11 C9                   ret
 955+ AB12
 956+ AB12              p127_physmem_trace_fill_text:
 957+ AB12 46 49 4C 4C          db      "FILL PAGE ID=",0
 957+ AB16 20 50 41 47
 957+ AB1A 45 20 49 44
 957+ AB1E 3D 00
 958+ AB20              p127_physmem_trace_verify_text:
 959+ AB20 56 45 52 49          db      "VERIFY PAGE ID=",0
 959+ AB24 46 59 20 50
 959+ AB28 41 47 45 20
 959+ AB2C 49 44 3D 00
 960+ AB30              p127_physmem_trace_hex_table:
 961+ AB30 30 31 32 33          db      "0123456789ABCDEF"
 961+ AB34 34 35 36 37
 961+ AB38 38 39 41 42
 961+ AB3C 43 44 45 46
 962+ AB40
 963+ AB40              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AB40              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AB40              p127_physmem_set_w3:
 966+ AB40 32 85 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_arg),a
 967+ AB43 CD B0 83             call    p127_map_encode_page
 968+ AB46 D8                   ret     c
 969+ AB47 01 F7 FF             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT
 970+ AB4A ED 79                out     (c),a
 971+ AB4C 3A 85 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_arg)
 972+ AB4F 32 93 85             ld      (p127_map_shadow+3),a
 973+ AB52 B7                   or      a
 974+ AB53 C9                   ret
 975+ AB54
 976+ AB54              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AB54              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AB54              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AB54 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AB57 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AB5A              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AB5A 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AB5B 07                   rlca
 984+ AB5C AC                   xor     h
 985+ AB5D AA                   xor     d
 986+ AB5E 77                   ld      (hl),a
 987+ AB5F 23                   inc     hl
 988+ AB60 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AB61 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AB62 B1                   or      c
 991+ AB63 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AB65 C9                   ret
 993+ AB66
 994+ AB66              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AB66              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:
 996+ AB66 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 997+ AB69 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 998+ AB6C              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop:
 999+ AB6C 7D                   ld      a,l
1000+ AB6D 07                   rlca
1001+ AB6E AC                   xor     h
1002+ AB6F AA                   xor     d
1003+ AB70 BE                   cp      (hl)
1004+ AB71 20 08                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail
1005+ AB73 23                   inc     hl
1006+ AB74 0B                   dec     bc
1007+ AB75 78                   ld      a,b
1008+ AB76 B1                   or      c
1009+ AB77 20 F3                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop
1010+ AB79 B7                   or      a
1011+ AB7A C9                   ret
1012+ AB7B              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail:
1013+ AB7B 37                   scf
1014+ AB7C C9                   ret
